### v1.0 — Anti-pattern cleanups (A5 §6)

- [~] **AP-01..08** 8 anti-patterns from logic audit — silent WAL drops / unwrap_or-0 / misleading Result signatures / hardcoded table names / shared-resource-as-value / unobservable side effects / runtime-format SQL / test-without-integration. **Several already covered by M-01..M-06 + AR-01 above; remaining ~4 items here.** — 5d aggregated — **partial 2026-05-28 Session 28b**: ✅ **silent-WAL-drops sub-item closed** — converted all 6 `let _ = …append(…)` audit-frame drops to observable `warn!`-on-error (the codebase pattern at n8n handlers.rs:288/491): `cli/profile.rs` APPROVED+DECLINED profile-delta frames (×2), `n8n_api/handlers.rs` PROVIDER_REQUEST/RESPONSE/ERROR (×3), `n8n_api/server.rs` N8N_REQUEST "every attempt is durable" audit frame (×1). ✅ **runtime-format-SQL sub-item verified clean** — only 3 `format!("…{table}")` hits exist, all test-only with hardcoded table-name arrays (`&["idx_episode",…]`), zero attacker-controlled interpolation, no injection vector (table names can't be SQL-parameterized anyway). ✅ **unwrap_or-0 destructive-defaults sub-item closed** — silent-failure-hunter swept `src/` for `unwrap_or(0)`/bad-default fallbacks that change a DECISION (not benign timestamp/display). 4 real fixes + 1 false-positive caught by verify-against-code: (1) `memory/gc_task.rs` clock-fault `now_ns=0` → fail-safe `Err` (loop logs warn + retries, mirrors M-03 — converts a silent ineffective GC pass into an observable skip); (2) `providers/circuit_breaker.rs::restore_from_disk` `now=0` defeated the staleness filter (`now - last_seen` goes large-negative → every months-old breaker row restored as fresh → healthy providers nudged toward Open) → `Err` on clock fault, caller already handles non-fatally; (3) `media/audio.rs` codec `sample_rate=None`→`0` silently skipped 16 kHz resampling → Whisper got raw-rate audio → garbage transcript stored as truth → now fails with `Backend` error (the genuinely destructive one); (4) `channels/whatsapp_webhook.rs` non-numeric `timestamp`→`0` (1970 epoch, mis-orders/retention-drops) → skip the message (boundary validation, consistent with existing non-text/empty skips) + new `decode_skips_text_message_with_unparseable_timestamp` test. **FALSE POSITIVE (reverted):** `providers/quota.rs::now_unix()` — `roll_daily_counter` with `now=0` is provably a self-healing no-op (`curr_day=0` can never exceed `prev_day≥0`, so it never resets/corrupts; recovers on next real tick), so M-03's "destructive" shape does NOT apply here. clippy `-D warnings` clean; media::audio/circuit_breaker/quota/gc/whatsapp suites green. ✅ **hardcoded-table-names sub-item verified clean** — the only non-test interpolated-table query is `memory/dimension.rs:95` (`measure_tier`), whose 4 callers all pass compile-time string literals (`idx_episode`/`idx_consolidated`/`idx_longterm`/`idx_groundtruth`) and whose `text_col` is whitelist-derived; no user input reaches a table name. `cli/rollback.rs` already gates with an `ALLOWED_TABLES` whitelist. Centralizing literals into constants would be low-value churn against Rule-of-Three. ✅ **misleading-Result-signatures** — the flagged case was decay_task (closed as M-04); a broad sweep for `-> Result<T>` that never `Err`s is low-value (most are trait-conformance / future-proofing). **AP-rest now reduces to two non-quick items: shared-resource-as-value (≡ F4-02 RateLimiter refactor, tracked separately) + test-without-integration (open-ended — per-feature integration coverage, not a one-shot cleanup).** **Session 30 verify-first:** re-confirmed AP-01..AP-07 ALL shipped against code (triage file:line evidence: profile.rs/n8n_api warn-guards, audio/circuit_breaker/gc_task fail-safe `Err`s, decay_task `()` signature, rollback `ALLOWED_TABLES`, rate_limit stateless `refill_and_consume` + single shared Arc, dispatcher 0x77 KANBAN_TASK_PROGRESS). Sole open sub-item is **AP-08** (test-without-integration) — open-ended per-feature integration coverage, deliberately NOT a one-shot. Stays `[~]` for AP-08 alone. **AP-08 verify-crypto e2e slice SHIPPED 2026-06-05 Session 39** — `run_verify_e2e_redaction_exemption_signature_trust` (in `cli/verify.rs` tests) drives the PUBLIC `run_verify(VerifyArgs)` entry end-to-end over the Session-39 0xF3 signature closure: builds a real HMAC-chained segment + COMPACTION_MARKER (single-encoder manual build — sidesteps the writer-reopen tangle the V02-03 note documents), redacts frame 1 via the REAL `redact::redact_frame_in_place` primitive (zero payload + REDACTED flag + CRC recompute → segment stays walkable, marker HMAC mismatches), then asserts the 4-step trust ladder: **intact → PASS; redacted+no-auth → FAIL; ATTACKER-signed 0xF3 → still FAIL (`operator_authorised_redactions:0` — the bypass that was open); OPERATOR-signed 0xF3 → reclassified PASS (`authorised_redaction_count:1`).** This is the public-entry counterpart to the function-level `extract_redaction_authorisations` test — the first e2e through the actual CLI verify entry (the V02-03 note's gap). verify suite 15/15 + clippy clean. AP-08 stays `[~]` (per-feature coverage is ongoing; the highest-value security path now has its e2e). **SEC-VERIFY-COMPRESS — the Session-39 STILL-OPEN-HIGH (`neoth verify` blind to compressed segments) CLOSED 2026-06-06 Session 39 (continued).** The gap: a finalized COMPRESSED (`wal.compression: zstd3`) segment is `v2-header(61) + zstd-blob`, with the COMPACTION_MARKER frames + the HMAC'd windows INSIDE the blob at LOGICAL offsets. `verify_marker` seeked RAW file bytes + `extract_markers`/0xF3/0xD9 scanners hardcoded `SEGMENT_HEADER_LEN`(60) + never decompressed → `extract_markers` found 0 markers → a SILENT "clean" verdict that checked NOTHING (the tamper-evidence guarantee silently void whenever compression is on — latent because compression defaults to `none`). **Offset model verified clean** (no header-shift): when compression is on the LIVE segment is ALREADY v2 (`state.offset` starts at 61), so marker offsets need no remap — logical = `header || decompress(blob)`. Fix: new `compaction::logical_segment_bytes(raw) -> (header_len, Cow<[u8]>)` (decompress-aware, tolerant of headerless test fixtures → offset 0; a flagged-compressed blob that won't inflate is a tamper-SUSPECT Err not a skip) + `compaction::verify_marker_bytes(logical, key, marker)` (HMAC over the slice). All four scanners (`extract_markers`→`extract_markers_from`, `extract_redaction_authorisations`, `segment_has_rotation`, `verify_marker`) + `verify_segments` now reconstruct logical bytes first; `verify_segments` decompresses ONCE per segment + surfaces an unreconstructable compressed segment as a FAILURE (no more silent false-clean). 2 new tests (`verify_marker_works_on_compressed_segment` clean-PASS + tamper-FAIL via the real `verify_marker` path; `run_verify_finds_and_checks_markers_in_a_compressed_segment` proves the public entry finds + checks a marker inside the blob) + the 2 pre-existing headerless-fixture tests restored by the tolerant fallback. compaction+verify 32/32, memory_wal_rotation + wal_emit_sites green, clippy `-D warnings` clean.

### v1.0 — Framework 4.1 reconciliation (A3 + A5)

New `ecology/correlation_detector.rs` (deterministic + LLM-free per the design pin): pure `detect_winner_streaks(records, min_streak)` (maximal consecutive-same-`provider` runs ≥ threshold — a low-dissent fitness signal) + `scan_winner_records(wal_dir)` (tolerant multi-segment WAL walk over `0x63 COUNCIL_WINNER_SELECTED`, `depth==0` outer-council only; reuses the proven council-walker primitives `parse_segment_header`/`decompress_frames`/`decode_frame`). New `freedom.yaml::ecology { enabled:false, correlation_min_streak:5 }` (default OFF). 9 tests (streak detection: alternating/maximal/two-separate/threshold/floor/empty + payload outer-only + scan missing-dir + **end-to-end real-WAL** write→scan→detect). default lib + clippy + docgen green. **PHASE 1 AUTO-SCHEDULER SHIPPED 2026-06-03 Session 37** — new `ecology/scheduler.rs`: `run_ecology_tick_once` scans the WAL for a low-dissent regime (winner streak ≥ `ecology.correlation_min_streak`) and on a fire re-aggregates the behavioural snapshot + STAGES P-04 self-dev proposals via the shared review-gated `cli::self_dev::propose_and_store` path (status `Pending`, idempotent, **never auto-applied**), emitting `0x4C ECOLOGY_SCHEDULER_FIRED {streak_signals_count, proposals_queued, ts_unix}` as the audit trail. **Honors the DESIGN_CH13 P2 HARD CONSTRAINT** (fitness must never silently rewrite policy): both halves hold — review-gated (proposals await `neoth self-dev accept`) AND WAL-audited (0x4C). `spawn_ecology_cron_loop` gated on `ecology.enabled` (default OFF) at 6h cadence (`scheduler_interval_secs`, 60s floor); wired into `serve.rs` (spawn + shutdown-abort). 7 new tests (spawn none/some + no-wal + below-threshold-no-fire + above-threshold-fires-and-emits-audit-frame disk-verify + serde-default-disabled + interval-clamp); 0x4C across all 5 WAL sites (SC-01a emit-site guard green). default lib 6807/0 + clippy + docgen + no_outbound_network green. **PHASE 3 TOOL-GENEALOGY SHIPPED 2026-06-03 Session 37** — new `ecology/genealogy.rs` + `neoth ecology genealogy [--wal-dir] [--home] [--top N]` (read-only, table+JSON). `build_tool_genealogy(wal_dir, installed_skill_ids)` walks the WAL frames that **actually carry tool identity** — `0xC0 MCP_TOOL_CALLED` (McpServer nodes `server_id/tool`), `0xC4 PLUGIN_HOSTCALL` + `0xC6 PLUGIN_CAP_USED` (Plugin use-count), `0xC2 PLUGIN_LOADED` (establishes idle plugin nodes) — plus installed skills as honest 0-count nodes (skill injection is NOT WAL-traced, only `0x29` skips). `ToolKind{Skill,Plugin,McpServer}` snake_case + `ToolNode{tool_id,kind,use_count,last_used_unix}` + `top_tools(n)` (count-desc, stable tie-break). **VERIFY-FIRST CORRECTION:** the blueprint's "winner-chain correlation per tool" + co-occurrence edges + `0x4D`/`0x4E` events were NOT built — `0x63` winner frames carry no tool/skill id and `0xC0` no session id, so those linkages would be *fabricated*, not measured (violates the CH-13 LLM-free/real-data pin). They are precursor-gated on adding tool-id to the council winner frame (a council-layer change), documented in the module. **No new WAL event** (read-only diagnostic, like the correlation scanner). NOT a `neoth doctor` subcommand — doctor is flag-based; the right home is the `neoth ecology` surface alongside `correlation`/`channel-weights`. 7 tests (empty + mcp-count + plugin hostcall+cap + skills-zero-count + top-sorted + malformed-no-panic + serde-pin). default lib 6814/0 + clippy + docgen (`docs/cli-commands.md`) green. **WINNER-CHAIN (measurable half) SHIPPED 2026-06-05 Session 39** — new `ecology/winner_chain.rs` + `neoth ecology winner-chain [--wal-dir] [--top N]` (read-only, table+JSON). `build_winner_chain(records)` is a PURE aggregator over the existing `scan_winner_records`: per-`(provider, role)` voice → `{wins, win_share, avg_score, last_score}` + the selection-mode mix (`by_mode`) + `total_debates`. Distinct from the `correlation` STREAK detector — this is the cumulative win-distribution + score-quality, not adjacency. **NO FABRICATION — every field (provider/role/score/mode) is literally on the `0x63` frame.** Required one additive `WinnerRecord.mode` field (the frame already carried `mode`; default `"unknown"` for pre-mode frames). 5th `neoth ecology` surface row + maturity matrix updated (now 5). 5 winner_chain tests (empty / aggregate-share+avg+last / same-provider-diff-role-distinct / mode-mix-sorted-desc / deterministic-tie-break) + correlation_detector mode-capture pins. default lib 6980/0 + clippy + docgen green. **STILL OPEN (precursor-gated, multi-session):** only the TOOL→win half — winner-frame tool-id plumbing → then per-tool genealogy edges (would need a new `0xEC`-band ecology event if ever surfaced as an apply-action; the speculative `0x4D/0x4E` naming above is void — `0x4D` is now `WORKER_DIED`/MONITOR-02 and ecology events live in the `0xEC`/`0xED`/`0xEE` band); `neoth ecology pause/resume`.

PF-01 makes semantic routing the DEFAULT: new `freedom.yaml::skills.always_embed_route: bool` (default `true`) — when set, Stage-2 runs on EVERY turn and a Stage-2 hit (cosine ≥ threshold) takes precedence over the keyword match; Stage-1 remains the fallback when Stage-2 returns None. **Cost is self-gating:** Stage-2 only fires at all when the operator configured `inference.embedding_provider` (off by default), so a default install pays nothing; for operators who opted into an embedding provider, "embeddings are the primary router" is the sensible reading of having configured one. `SkillsConfig` gained a manual `Default` impl (the field defaults `true` consistently for both `::default()` and a freedom.yaml that omits it — pinned by a round-trip test). The routing log now carries `overrode_keyword` so an operator can see when a semantic match displaced a keyword hit. Set `always_embed_route: false` to restore the keyword-miss-only fallback. **GUI toggle rolls into GU-01** (settings-parity audit). Verified: full `cargo test -p neothd` exit 0 (lib 6559/0 + caldav + `no_outbound_network` guard + docgen), clippy clean. ✅ **CalDAV add/close WRITE SHIPPED 2026-06-03 Session 36 — full 6-safety-set, NOT a bare PUT.** New `tools::caldav` write fns (pure core unit-tested + wiremock round-trips): `build_vtodo_ics` (RFC-5545 CRLF, ICS-escaped SUMMARY) + `set_status_completed` (rewrite-in-place, preserves SUMMARY/DUE) + `task_uid` (deterministic `neoth-<xxh3>` → the idempotency key) + `validate_uid` (path-traversal guard) + `resource_url`. **(1) permission gate** — new `permissions::Action::ExternalTaskWrite{provider,action}` routed through `evaluate` (Strict/Standard **Confirm**, Elevated/Full **Allow**; unleasable) + a `gate_external_task_write` consumer (`--yes` / interactive-TTY `confirm_interactive` / else bail — no silent network write). **(2) WAL event** — new `0xC8 TODO_WRITE` (tool band; const+doc+band-assert+both name-tables; best-effort one-shot emit, daemon-live-skip; metadata only — provider/action/uid/summary, never creds). **(3) `--dry-run`** — prints the would-be write, sends nothing, emits nothing. **(4) idempotency** — create PUTs with `If-None-Match: *`; a re-run on the same deterministic UID returns `412 → CreateOutcome::AlreadyExists` (no duplicate), not an error. **(5) ETag/`If-Match`** — close GETs the live resource + its ETag, sets STATUS:COMPLETED, PUTs back with `If-Match: <etag>`; a concurrent server edit yields `412 → CloseOutcome::Conflict` (surfaced to the operator, never a silent clobber). **(6) confirm in standard autonomy** — the gate above. 12 new caldav tests (5 pure incl. escape + status-rewrite-preserves-rest, 5 wiremock incl. 201-created / 412-idempotent / 404-not-found / etag-complete / 412-conflict, + uid-determinism + traversal-reject) + 1 permission-policy test. `neoth todo --provider caldav {add,close}` live; module + `Caldav` docs un-stale-d; docgen regenerated for the new `--dry-run`/`--yes` flags. lib 6675/0, clippy 0. ✅ **MICROSOFT TO DO SHIPPED 2026-06-03 Session 36 — TD-02 FULLY CLOSED.** New `tools::microsoft_todo` (MS Graph), mirroring the google_tasks `_against`+wiremock shape: OAuth `refresh_token` grant against `login.microsoftonline.com/{tenant}/oauth2/v2.0/token` (tenant defaults `common`; scope `Tasks.ReadWrite offline_access`; access tokens never persisted; error surfaces only HTTP status — no refresh-token leak) + `list_tasks`/`create_task`/`close_task` over `graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/me/todo`. Unlike Google's `@default`, MS Graph has no default-list alias — each op first resolves the default list (`wellknownListName == defaultList`) then operates on `/lists/{id}/tasks`. `MicrosoftTask{id,title,status,dueDateTime}` + `is_open()`; `validate_id` path-injection guard. `neoth todo --provider microsoft {list,add,close}`; 4 new `credentials.yaml::ms_todo_{tenant_id,client_id,client_secret,refresh_token}` fields (+`NEOTH_MS_TODO_*` env) wired into `is_empty`. 10 tests. No new deps. lib 6697/0, clippy 0. **TD-02 (Todoist + Google Tasks + CalDAV read+write + Microsoft To Do) is now end-to-end complete across all four backends.** ✅ **P0 UNIFIED ExternalTaskWrite GATE + TODO_WRITE-via-audit-RPC SHIPPED 2026-06-03 Session 36 (operator-flagged: "CalDAV sichtbar gegated, Todoist/Google nicht").** Previously only the CalDAV path called `gate_external_task_write` + `--dry-run` + `0xC8 TODO_WRITE`; todoist/google/microsoft writes were UNGATED + UNAUDITED. Now `run_todo` centralizes the gate for ALL four backends BEFORE dispatch: every `add`/`close` on any provider runs the SAME `ExternalTaskWrite{provider,action}` autonomy/consent gate (Strict/Standard Confirm via `--yes`/TTY/else-bail, Elevated/Full Allow) + `enforce_required_audit` pre-flight (live daemon + unreachable listener + `required_for_oneshot_permission_events` → REFUSE the write) + a unified `--dry-run` (CalDAV still shows its deterministic `task_uid`; the REST backends show the target as the uid placeholder). `emit_todo_write` is now **async** and FORWARDS `0xC8 TODO_WRITE` over the loopback audit-RPC channel when a daemon owns the WAL (was: silent daemon-live-skip) — every successful todoist/google/microsoft/caldav add+close now emits its audit frame whether or not `neoth serve` is running. `0xC8` added to `ALLOWED_CLIENT_EVENT_TYPES`. ### v1.0 — Hermes adopts (clarify + goal)

- [x] **CL-01** Clarify-prompt interrupt — mid-turn open question via SSE, 120s timeout. — 2.5d (hermes adopt) ✅ **RESOLVED 2026-06-03 Session 36 (panel rank-9, verify-first reasoned closure — same root cause as GM-01).** CL-01's interactive 'interrupt before the provider call, ask the operator, wait 120s for an answer, resume' is an ARCHITECTURAL MISMATCH with NEOTH's model: `cli/chat.rs:8` is explicit — **'No interactive REPL'** + 'single-turn chat reply'. `neoth chat "prompt"` is ONE-SHOT (the operator passed the prompt + isn't sitting at a TTY) — a blocking `ask_clarify_cli` stdin-read would HANG a one-shot command waiting for an answer that never comes (a bug, not a feature). The channel/serve path handles each inbound INDEPENDENTLY (no stateful mid-turn pause/resume) — the operator's 'answer' arrives as a SEPARATE inbound, not a resumption; building pause/resume is a deep stateful refactor. The only NEOTH-fitting alternative (a deterministic keyword ambiguity classifier → inject an 'ask-for-clarification' system-prompt nudge) is MARGINAL: the LLM already asks for clarification on ambiguous/under-specified requests natively (a keyword heuristic is a weaker duplicate of model judgment) and it costs 8-site EnrichmentInputs churn for behaviour the model already exhibits. **PREREQUISITE for a real CL-01 (tracked):** an interactive `neoth chat` REPL (no-prompt-arg → stdin loop) OR a stateful channel mid-turn-pause/resume layer — only then is the clarify-interrupt a genuine consumer. **Systemic finding: hermes-adopts (GM-01, CL-01) assume an agentic multi-turn loop NEOTH deliberately doesn't have for chat; verify-first closes them honestly instead of building primitive-ahead/harmful code.**
- [x] **GM-01** Goal manager + turn budget — self-terminate when goal satisfied. — 3d (hermes adopt) ✅ **RESOLVED 2026-06-03 Session 36 (panel rank-8, verify-first).** The TURN-BUDGET half is now operator-tunable + the GOAL-SATISFACTION half is closed as an architectural mismatch. **Verify-first found:** NEOTH chat is single-turn request-response (no agentic multi-turn session to 'self-terminate'), and the ONE real iterative loop (MCP autoroute `dispatch_loop::run_tool_loop`) ALREADY had a turn budget (`DEFAULT_MAX_ITERATIONS=5` + `hit_cap`). A keyword-overlap 'goal satisfied' heuristic wired into that tool-loop would risk PREMATURE task termination → harmful, not helpful → NOT built (would be primitive-ahead + dangerous). **Shipped (the genuine deliverable):** the dispatch-loop cap is now operator-tunable via `freedom.yaml::goal.max_turns` (new `GoalConfig`, default 5 = no behaviour change) — threaded `run_mcp_dispatch_loop`→`run_tool_loop_with_cap` on BOTH the `neoth chat` + channel-serve paths (via `PipelineHandlerDeps.goal_max_turns`). 1 config test (default-5 / absent-block / explicit). lib 6792/0, clippy 0. The hermes 'goal manager + classify_goal_satisfaction' stays unbuilt by design (mismatch with NEOTH's request-response model; the coding-workflow already has its own `EarlyStopDetector`).

### v1.0 — Final four killer features (A6)

- [x] **KF-03** Tamper-Evidence Export — signed `.neoth-proof` bundle for any WAL window. **Merges with A3 #7 `neoth wal export --signed`.** — 3d — ✅ **UNSIGNED slice SHIPPED 2026-06-01 Session 32** (verify-first triage gremium). `wal/proof_bundle.rs` (NEW): `ProofFrame` (segment + logical offset + event_type/id/ts_ns + **payload_hash** [per-frame anchor] + payload_len + importance; payload BYTES deliberately omitted — proves integrity without exfiltrating message content/PII), `ProofMarker` (a `0x15 COMPACTION_MARKER` copied verbatim so a verifier re-runs the daemon's own `compaction::verify_marker` over the sealed range), `ProofBundle` (`canonical_bytes()` = stable sign-ready encoding + `digest_hex()` SHA-256), **`ProofEnvelope`** (`seal()` writes the bundle + `digest_sha256` over canonical_bytes [self-integrity, checked before trusting frames] + `signature: None` slot). `cli/wal.rs`: **`neoth wal export --window <24h|7d|RFC3339..RFC3339> [--out] [--verify-chain] [--wal-dir]`** — `parse_window` (duration via `parse_duration` OR half-open RFC3339 range), `collect_proof` (robust v1/v2 segment walk, window-filtered frames + all compaction markers), `--verify-chain` re-runs `verify_marker` per marker → `chain_verified` (true ONLY if ≥1 marker AND all verify; **honest**: false when window not yet sealed — per-frame `payload_hash` still anchors). Atomic tmp+rename write to `~/.neoth/exports/`. **HONEST scope:** the bundle proves chain + per-frame integrity; `chain_verified` never overclaims. **SIGN-READY:** `canonical_bytes()` is what `--sign` will minisign verbatim into `signature`. **✅ `--sign` SLICE SHIPPED 2026-06-02 Session 34 — KF-03 FULLY CLOSED.** The minisign-keypair blocker DISSOLVED: a 3-lens DAU-safety gremium (unanimous) chose **NEOTH-native ed25519** over minisign-shell-out (DAU-hostile: install + keygen + password; not CI-testable) or pure-Rust-minisign-format (scrypt-seckey reimpl risk). The operator's per-proof signing key is **auto-managed** — generated + persisted on first `neoth wal export --sign` exactly like the WAL HMAC key (`wal/signing.rs::load_or_init_signing_key` mirrors `compaction::load_or_init_key`: 32-byte ed25519 seed via `getrandom`, fail-closed, `~/.neoth/wal/signing.key`, unix 0600 / Windows DPAPI-wrap + DACL via the shared `write_key_securely`/`maybe_unwrap_dpapi` now `pub(crate)`). Zero install, zero prompt, zero crypto knowledge — DAU-safe. **`ProofEnvelope` → schema v2**: `signature` = b64 ed25519 over `canonical_bytes()`, + `signer_pubkey` (b64, embedded so verification is self-contained) + `sig_algorithm="ed25519-raw"`; `sign(&SigningKey)` + `check_signature(expected)` returning a typed `SignatureCheck` that **distinguishes SelfConsistent (embedded key — proves no post-sign tamper, NOT identity) from VerifiedAgainstExpected (operator's out-of-band-pinned `--pubkey` — true attribution)** so a verifier never mistakes one for the other (the gremium's key security caveat). **Consumer = new `neoth wal verify-proof --proof <f> [--pubkey <b64>]`** (digest-check first, then ed25519; ONE plain-language verdict + exit-code — DAU-readable). `ed25519-dalek 2` promoted transitive→direct (0 new crates). 13 new tests (6 signing: round-trip/tamper/wrong-key/malformed-b64/malformed-seed/generate-stable + 6 proof_bundle: sign-fields/self-consistent/verified-against-expected/wrong-expected/post-sign-tamper/unsigned-roundtrip + 1 CLI verify round-trip, hermetic ephemeral keys). **No WAL event needed** (`0xF4` stays unreserved — signing is a CLI export action, no daemon frame). Verified: full `cargo test -p neothd` exit 0 (incl. docgen `cli_commands_md_is_up_to_date` regenerated for `--sign`/`verify-proof`), clippy `--lib --tests -D warnings` clean. **Note:** this is the OPERATOR's per-proof key (separate trust root); the PROJECT release-signing key (MAR-02/MV-01b) is still a distinct, separate operator-CI action. 12 new tests incl. **envelope-digest tamper-detection** (flip a frame post-export → `digest_matches()` false) + export round-trip + window filtering + marker pickup. No new WAL event code (pure reader); `0xF4` informally reserved for the sign-slice audit frame. clippy + fmt clean; docs regenerated.
- [x] **KF-09** WASM Plugin Capability Ledger — per-plugin-per-session usage log via `plugins/capability_ledger.rs`. New WAL frame `0x60 PLUGIN_CAP_USED`. — 4.5d — **READ-CAPABILITY AUDIT FIRST-SLICE SHIPPED 2026-05-31 Session 31** (the gremium SECURITY lens's #1, deferred ONLY for the WAL-slot collision — now resolved). **Verify-first corrected the spec's `0x60`:** `0x60` is `EVENT_TYPE_COUNCIL_SYNTHESIS_ATTEMPTED` — the correct free slot is **`0xC6`** (tool band `0xC0..=0xCF`, the plugin-events neighborhood next to `0xC4 PLUGIN_HOSTCALL`/`0xC5 PLUGIN_FUEL_EXHAUSTED`). **The real gap closed:** `wasm_plugin/hostcalls.rs::recall_top` (a ReadOnly capability that reads operator memory by prompt-hash) emitted NO durable WAL frame — only a `tracing::debug`/`warn` — while the Write-side `emit_event` already emits `0xC4`. So a plugin could PROBE operator memory invisibly (exactly the security-lens threat: "a malicious plugin calls recall_top on every turn to exfiltrate memory with no signal"). Now every `recall_top` call emits a `0xC6 PLUGIN_CAP_USED` frame `{plugin, capability, prompt_hash (`{:016x}` — correlatable to the queried hash), hits}` via the `wal_writer` already present in `PluginStoreState` — **best-effort** (a WAL issue NEVER changes the read hint the plugin gets back; recall stays a pure hint) and **batchable** (NOT immediate-sync — higher-volume than the lifecycle frames + a lost tail on crash is acceptable for a read-audit). **Consumer is the universal one:** `neoth wal show --type plugin_cap_used` renders it (the name-registry entry makes it filterable) — so it's NOT primitive-ahead-of-consumer; an operator can now grep exactly when a plugin touched their memory. New `EVENT_TYPE_PLUGIN_CAP_USED = 0xC6` + band-membership const-assert + name-registry + uniqueness test + the SC-01a `wal_emit_sites` source-walk guard (confirms the const is WIRED at the recall_top emit site, not defined-dead). 3 payload-shape tests (stable JSON / unsigned-016x prompt_hash / determinism). Verified: clippy `--lib --tests` 0 warnings on default AND `wasm-plugin-host`, default lib **6265/0**, feature hostcalls 26 green, SC-01a green, fmt clean. **AGGREGATE-ROLLUP CONSUMER SHIPPED 2026-05-31 Session 31 (same session, the spec's "usage log" — KF-09 now CLOSED end-to-end):** new **`neoth plugin ledger [<id>]`** — a read-only consumer that scans every `*.wal` segment (robust v1/v2 + zstd-decompress, mirroring the SPEC-10 refusal-history walker) for the plugin audit frames — `0xC4 PLUGIN_HOSTCALL` WRITES (`emit_event`, summing `payload_bytes`) AND `0xC6 PLUGIN_CAP_USED` READS (`recall_top`, summing `hits`) — and aggregates a per-(plugin, capability) row `{calls, total_payload_bytes (write volume), total_hits (read volume)}`, optionally filtered to one plugin id. So an operator runs `neoth plugin ledger snoop` and SEES exactly what a plugin exercised (e.g. "recall_top × 412 calls, 1180 hits" = a plugin hammering memory reads). **Default-build consumer** (NOT `wasm-plugin-host`-gated) — it reads HISTORICAL frames, so even a slim daemon that can't run the wasm host can still audit what past plugins did. Pure-fn core (`parse_cap_frame` + `aggregate_ledger` BTreeMap-keyed for stable plugin-then-capability ordering) split from the WAL scan for unit-testing; tolerant throughout (missing dir / torn segment / malformed payload each skip). Table + JSON. 6 tests (3 `parse_cap_frame`: 0xC4-write / 0xC6-read / reject-other-type+garbage; 2 `aggregate_ledger`: counts-sums-sorts / filter-by-id; 1 END-TO-END real-WAL: writes 2×0xC6 + 1×0xC4 + 1 unrelated frame through the real `wal::writer::spawn`, asserts the ledger aggregates exactly the plugin frames with correct call/byte/hit sums + the unrelated one filtered). Verified: clippy `--lib --tests` 0 warnings on default AND `wasm-plugin-host`, full lib **6271/0**, fmt clean. **DEVIATIONS from the literal spec (deliberate):** the aggregator lives in `cli/plugin.rs` (a CLI read-consumer) not a separate `plugins/capability_ledger.rs` module (no producer-side state to house — the WAL IS the ledger store); the rollup is LIFETIME (whole-WAL) rather than strictly per-SESSION (more useful — a since-last-boot filter is a trivial ts-cutoff refinement if an operator asks). Extending the `0xC6` emit to future read hostcalls layers on the same `build_cap_used_payload` shape + flows into this same aggregator automatically.
- [x] **KF-10** Structured-Forgetting Export — pre-decay summary drafts to Obsidian `~/Obsidian/NEOTH/PreDecay/`. New WAL frame `0x94 MEMORY_PRE_DECAY_DRAFT`. — 3d — 🔧 **Core export shipped 2026-05-30 Session 30 (verify-first triage BUILD_NOW).** Just before the consolidation pass DELETEs a hot memory that has fallen below `FORGET_FLOOR` (forgotten forever — not promoted), it now drafts a frontmatter-markdown summary into `<obsidian_vault>/PreDecay/YYYY-MM-DD-{event_id}.md` — a last-chance reviewable record. **Design (verify-first beat the triage spec twice):** (1) the triage's criterion `importance < 2*FORGET_FLOOR` DIVERGED from the real deletion predicate (`< FORGET_FLOOR` at consolidate.rs:135); a re-query would export the wrong set — so the rows are **captured inline at the delete site** (the EXACT forgotten set, by construction). (2) the spec's `0x94` collides with the already-shipped `EVENT_TYPE_CONSOLIDATION_PASS` (0x94); and a NEW frame would require threading a `WalWriterHandle` through `decay_task::spawn` (which has none) while the consolidation deletion it shadows emits NO frame today — so the WAL frame is **deliberately deferred**: the `.md` files ARE the durable operator-visible record, and `PassReport.pre_decay_drafted` carries the count, ready to fold into a future consolidation audit frame. **Code:** new `memory::pre_decay_export` (atomic per-file tmp+rename, best-effort — a full/read-only vault can NEVER fail a decay pass; file IO is AFTER tx-commit so it never holds the DB lock) + `run_consolidation_pass` gained `vault_path: Option<&Path>` (default `None` = byte-for-byte pre-KF-10 behaviour; the hot path is untouched unless a vault is set) + `PassReport.pre_decay_drafted` + `decay_task::{spawn,run,run_once}` thread the vault + `serve.rs` passes `config.obsidian_vault`. 10 tests (8 module: empty/frontmatter/count/filename/atomic-no-tmp/dir-create/overwrite-not-dup/extreme-ts-no-panic + 2 consolidate integration: only-the-forgotten-set-drafted incl. a consolidated row NOT drafted / None-is-a-no-op). A no-op pass (`pass_did_work` false) writes nothing — no audit noise. `0x94` was already SC-01a-"wired" via the `cli/events.rs` registry reference, so no RESERVED change; the stale aspirational means-payload doc-comment was corrected to match the real `PassReport` fields. 2 new tests (seeded-row → 1 frame / empty-db → 0 frames); both existing tests updated to the new `run_once`/`spawn` arity. lib 6648/0, clippy 0. **Adversarial-review follow-up (4-lens, 15 findings → 4 confirmed, 11 refuted):** the KF-10 hot-path lens surfaced 2 PRE-EXISTING decay-task bugs (fixed): **(HIGH)** `decay_task::run_once` clamped a far-future-clock nanosecond-overflow to `i64::MAX` (`try_from(...).unwrap_or(i64::MAX)`) → `seven_days_ago = i64::MAX - 7d` makes EVERY event look >7d old → mass hot-tier consolidate+delete (M-03 redux under a year-2262 clock trigger, violating the M-03 comment's own "skip on clock failure" intent) — now the overflow arm refuses the pass + `tracing::error!` exactly like the pre-epoch arm; **(MEDIUM)** the decay ticker lacked `set_missed_tick_behavior(Skip)` (the codebase-wide pattern — auto_update/doctor_cron/drift_alert_cron/cron::scheduler all set it) → a pass outrunning the 2h interval could double-run back-to-back and forget rows that only just crossed FORGET_FLOOR during the first pass's decay UPDATE, WITHOUT a pre-decay draft — now set. (Refuted, with reasoning: the Phase-3/4 warm/cold drops are intentionally out of KF-10 scope — they keep their archive `.md`; the `pre_decay_drafted` vs `hot_archived` comparison is already operator-derivable; negative-event_id filenames are unreachable.)
- [x] **A3-01** `neoth transfer --dest <pubkey>` signed encrypted memory bundle. — A3 #1 — 3.5d — 🔧 **EXPORT COMMAND + bidirectional crypto SHIPPED + VERIFIED 2026-06-03 Session 36 (verify-first triage BUILDABLE_NOW); end-to-end needs the keygen + import slices below.** Hybrid-crypto memory export to another NEOTH instance. New `memory/transfer_bundle.rs` (PURE crypto core, no I/O, fully unit-testable): `encrypt_for(payload, dest_x25519_pub, &SigningKey, ts)` = ephemeral **X25519 ECDH** against the recipient's pubkey → **HKDF-SHA256** (salt = ephemeral pubkey, info `neoth-transfer-v1`) → one-shot **AES-256-GCM** key → encrypt; then **ed25519-sign** `canonical_bytes()` with the operator's existing `wal::signing` key. `decrypt_with(bundle, recipient_x25519_secret)` verifies the signature (self-consistent) then ECDHs + GCM-decrypts. `TransferBundle` is all-base64 → JSON-round-trips; the PLAINTEXT never appears (only `ciphertext_b64` + ephemeral pubkey + nonce persist). `verify_signature(bundle, Some(&pinned_pubkey))` distinguishes **SelfConsistent** (embedded key — no post-sign tamper, NOT identity) from **VerifiedAgainstExpected** (operator-pinned — true attribution), mirroring KF-03's signature-check taxonomy. New dep `x25519-dalek = {2, features=["static_secrets"]}` (RustCrypto, pure-Rust, MSVC-clean; HKDF/AES-GCM/ed25519/sha2/base64/getrandom all already present; not banned in deny.toml). New `cli/transfer.rs` **`neoth transfer --dest <b64> [--out] [--days] [--dry-run]`** — validates the dest pubkey (base64→[u8;32]), collects the last `--days` (default 7) of hot-tier `idx_episode` rows as a JSON dump (`collect_from_conn`, pure + unit-tested against an in-memory db; missing views.db → empty), loads the operator signing key, seals the bundle, atomic-writes it to `~/.neoth/exports/transfer-<unix>.json` (or `--out`), and emits **`0xF5 MEMORY_TRANSFER_EXPORTED`** (metadata-only: dest + bundle_bytes + events_exported + window, NEVER plaintext). `--dry-run` shows the plan (events/size/recipient) with no write + no audit. Emit uses the `neoth dream now` one-shot-writer + daemon-live-skip pattern (avoids racing the daemon's segment). New WAL `0xF5` in the 0xF0 band (const + doc + band-assert + both name-tables; `needs_immediate_sync` default-true → an export auto-syncs). `Commands::Transfer` wired in `cli/mod.rs`; docgen `docs/cli-commands.md` regenerated. 14 new tests (9 crypto: round-trip / wrong-recipient-key / tampered-ciphertext-fails-GCM / post-sign-tamper-fails-sig / verify-against-expected / wrong-expected-rejected / parse-b64-32-length / canonical-stable-excludes-sig / json-round-trip + 5 CLI: window-dump / window-exclusion / missing-db-empty / atomic-write / **end-to-end collect→encrypt→decrypt recovers the memory dump**). Verified: full `cargo test -p neothd` exit 0 (lib **6662/0** incl. SC-01a `every_event_type_constant_is_wired` for 0xF5 + event-uniqueness + docgen + no_outbound), clippy `--lib --tests -D warnings` 0. ✅ **A3-01 HARDENED + CLOSED 2026-06-03 Session 36 (operator-flagged: "Export alone is half a product").** (1) **Audit-RPC for 0xF5** — `0xF5 MEMORY_TRANSFER_EXPORTED` added to the `ALLOWED_CLIENT_EVENT_TYPES` allowlist (18 codes); `transfer export` now FORWARDS the frame over the loopback audit-RPC channel when a daemon owns the WAL (was: skipped) + an `enforce_required_audit` pre-flight REFUSES the export if required-audit is on + the daemon's listener is unreachable. (2) **Managed X25519 receiving keypair** — `transfer_bundle::load_or_init_transfer_key` (`~/.neoth/wal/transfer.key`, auto-generated, 0600/DPAPI, mirrors the signing key) + `transfer_pubkey_b64`; surfaced via **`neoth identity pubkey`** (share it as a sender's `--dest`). (3) **Receive side** — `neoth transfer` restructured into subcommands: **`export`** (the hardened sealer) / **`verify <bundle> [--pubkey]`** / **`inspect <bundle>`** (metadata, no decrypt) / **`import <bundle> [--pubkey]`** (decrypt with the managed key → recover the memory dump to `~/.neoth/imports/`). (4) **Five distinct verify verdicts** — `transfer_bundle::verify_bundle` → `SelfConsistent` (embedded-key only, no identity proof) / `VerifiedAgainstExpected` (operator-pinned sender) / `SignatureMismatch` / `WrongRecipient` (dest ≠ my pubkey) / `UnsupportedSchema(v)`; `verify` exits non-zero on any non-OK verdict (script-gateable). (5) **Size caps** — new `freedom.yaml::transfer { max_events:1000, max_plaintext_bytes:8MiB, max_bundle_bytes:16MiB }`; export refuses a runaway BEFORE encrypting + after sealing; `read_bundle` refuses an oversized file before allocating; import re-checks the recovered plaintext cap. 10 new tests (5 crypto: 5-verdict matrix + keypair-gen+persist+derive; 5 CLI: collect-window×2 / missing-db / atomic-write / oversized-bundle-refused / verdict_str / export→verify→import e2e). lib 6704/0, clippy 0, docgen regenerated. **FOLLOW-ON (own slice): merging an imported dump into the LIVE recall store** (dedup + provenance + identity attribution) — `import` recovers to a file today; the merge-into-views.db step needs its own dedup/provenance design.

Falls back to fresh download on any staged-apply failure. 4 tests (install-without-network + tamper-refused + pending round-trip + path). **serve.rs RESTART CONTRACT SHIPPED 2026-05-29 Session 28c** (the earlier "dead-code" concern resolved by a cross-process signal-file design): `daemon/supervisor::{restart_request_path, request_restart, take_restart_request}` (`~/.neoth/restart.request` marker) + a serve.rs watcher task (5s poll) + an `Arc<Notify>` + a third `select!` arm → graceful drain → exit → supervisor relaunches onto the new binary. The trigger is the manual apply path: `run_self_apply`'s `maybe_request_restart()` writes the marker after a successful swap **iff `config.supervisor.enabled`** (no supervisor → no marker, since an exit would just leave the daemon down). **Stop vs restart**: stop is the supervisor's own command (`neoth supervisor uninstall` / `systemctl --user stop` / Task end), NOT an exit code — so no global SIGTERM-exit-code change was needed. 6050 lib tests green (no regression from the select! surgery), restart round-trip test added. **Option-A neoth-self is now FULLY COMPLETE** — gate + signing-verify + supervisor (module+CLI+wizard) + 0xD2 enrichment + staging task + manual fast-path + restart contract all shipped. SUPERVISOR — new `daemon/supervisor.rs` (systemd user-unit+linger / launchd LaunchAgent / Windows Task-Scheduler + a built-in `neoth supervisor-loop` subcommand), exit-code 0=restart/2=stop via the existing shutdown drain + a RestartRequested Notify, wizard step7d + `SupervisorConfig`; ship Linux+macOS first (~3d), Windows follow (~3d). Remaining prereqs: #2 sigstore-verify, #3 supervisor, #4 0xD2-daemon-emit-fix + richer payload, #5 the unattended task gated by all of the above. **RELEASE.YML WINDOWS-`.zip` COSIGN GAP CLOSED 2026-05-29 Session 28d (gremium quality-lens v1.0 blocker):** the release `Sign archives (keyless)` step looped only over `*.tar.gz`, so every Windows `.zip` artifact (`x86_64`/`aarch64-pc-windows-msvc`) shipped **unsigned** — and the GitHub-release `files:` upload glob omitted `dist/*.zip` entirely, so the Windows archives were never even attached to the release. Fixed both: the sign loop is now `shopt -s nullglob; for f in *.tar.gz *.zip; do cosign sign-blob --yes "$f" --bundle "${f}.cosign.bundle"; done` (Unix tarballs **and** Windows zips both get a `.cosign.bundle`) + added `dist/*.zip` to the release `files:` list so the Windows artifacts upload alongside their bundles + checksums. ## Sequencing rules (carry-over + new from A2)

1. **Each lane ships independently.** v0.2.1 hotfix MUST tag before any v0.3 work merges. v0.3 must tag and be public-ready before v0.4 work starts on `main`.
2. **WAL frame band reservations** — claim event-type IDs in this file BEFORE implementation. v0.3 claims `0x11`, `0x12 extend`, `0x15`, `0x80..83`, `0x05/06`. v0.4 claims `0x30`. v0.9 claims `0x19/1A`, `0x50/51`, `0x60`, `0x78/79`, `0x94`, `0xA4/A5/A6`, `0xE8`. v1.0 claims final cleanup band. Coordinate via this file before any new WAL event lands.
3. **Wizard is the activation surface for every lane.** Every new operator-facing feature ships with a wizard step + a settings-panel entry (GUI ↔ CLI parity hard rule).
4. **Security blockers are pre-merge.** PC-02 Chrome DevTools MCP requires env-var force + license confirm BEFORE the PR opens, not in v1.0 hardening. CRIT-01/02/03 from A5 must ship in v0.2.1 hotfix lane BEFORE any v0.3 work merges.
5. **A2 critical sequencing dependencies:**
   - W-05 (installer trait) ships before U-01/U-03
   - M-07 (memory tests) → M-09 (region routing) → OM-01 (OMI ingest)
   - P-01+P-02 (approval tiers) → PC-01 (OS tool surface) — sonst brutale confirm fatigue
   - G-01 with `G-01a ProactiveQueue` → PL-03 / OB-03 — sonst notification storm
   - OB-01 (dreaming→vault) → G-01 (trigger) → N-06 (workflow delivery)
   - W-03 with `operator_complexity_level()` → GU-03 (panel collapse rule engine)
   - AR-05 `neoth profile conflicts` → G-02 (proactive surfacing wrong claims)
6. **No "next-version" debt creep.** Items deferred from a lane move to the explicit `_post-v1.0_` column above, not silently into the next lane.

---

## How to use this file

- Pick the highest-priority lane that's not yet shipped.
- Pick an item from the top of that lane's section.
- Flip `[ ]` → `[~]` in the same commit you start work.
- Ship code + tests + same-turn `[~]` → `[x]` flip with commit hash + 1-line evidence.
- Push.
- Repeat.

- [x] **ADV-01** WAL `.cpt` crash-recovery files use only CRC32c + xxh3-64 (non-cryptographic). Attacker pre-places crafted `.cpt` with injected `PROFILE_DELTA` events. Add HMAC-SHA256 over `.cpt` content + verify before apply + re-run region_tag validation on recovered frames. — F4 — S 1-2d — ✅ 2026-05-25 Session 24. Implementation built per SPEC §4.3 since no prior `.cpt` infrastructure existed in code. Three new modules: `wal/cpt_auth.rs` (`CompactionAuthenticator` + HMAC-SHA256 sub-key derived from existing `wal/hmac.key` master via `HMAC(label, master)` with domain-separation label `b"neoth.wal.cpt.v1"`; vault-key integration deferred since vault is not yet wired for this purpose — swap key source when vault lands), `wal/cpt_format.rs` (7-step atomic-rename pair write: `.tmp` → fsync → `.hmac.tmp` → fsync → rename `.hmac.tmp → .hmac` → rename `.cpt.tmp → .cpt` → parent-dir fsync on unix; `read_and_verify_cpt` returns `WalError::CompactionAuthFailed` on tamper/missing/short-hmac/wrong-key; `quarantine_pair` renames with `.rejected.<ts_unix>` suffix), `wal/cpt_recovery.rs` (startup scan: walks `wal_dir/*.cpt`, applies-or-quarantines each pair, returns `ScanReport{applied, quarantined}`, `auth_failed_payload` builds JSON for the audit frame). Claimed `EVENT_TYPE_COMPACTION_AUTH_FAILED = 0x51` in recovery band (handoff said 0x18 = REFUSAL_REDIRECTED taken; SPEC said 0x40 = JOB_FIRED taken; 0x51 = first free next to RECOVERY_TRUNCATED at 0x50). New `WalError::CompactionAuthFailed { reason }` variant. 35 tests across the three modules (cross-key, tamper, missing-hmac, wrong-length hmac, all-zero-tag, atomic-rename invariant, quarantine timestamp suffix, mixed-dir half-good/half-tampered, scan skips non-cpt, event-code pin, JSON payload shape, drop-wipes sub-key, should_panic on short master). Daemon-boot wiring also shipped in this commit: `cli/serve.rs` step 2b loads master key + builds authenticator + runs `scan_and_apply` BEFORE writer spawn (so `.cpt → .bin` renames happen before any segment is opened); step 3b after writer spawn emits one `EVENT_TYPE_COMPACTION_AUTH_FAILED` (0x51) audit frame per quarantined pair so operators see rejections in `neoth wal show`. Fail-modes are best-effort: missing master key / scan I/O error logs `warn!` and continues startup; quarantine-emit failure logs warn but daemon keeps running.

New helper `circuit_breaker::run_with_breaker(provider_id, fut)` wraps the existing `acquire_for` + `record_success`/`record_failure` boilerplate so every provider gets a 1-line wire-in. All 7 `Provider::complete` impls wired: `openai_api.rs`, `gemini_api.rs`, `azure_openai.rs`, `aws_bedrock.rs`, `claude_cli.rs`, `local_qwen.rs`, `ouro/adapter.rs`. Pattern: wrap the existing `complete()` body in `run_with_breaker(name, async { ... }).await` — any `?` exit lands as `record_failure`, the final `Ok` lands as `record_success`. Open-circuit rejection surfaces as `anyhow!("circuit breaker open for {name}: ...")` so operators reading the WAL audit correlate. 3 new tests pin the helper: admit+success path keeps state Closed + resets consecutive_failures, propagate+failure path bumps tally without tripping, trip-after-6-failures rejects subsequent acquire without entering the future. **Stream-wrapping shipped Session 24 follow-up** (per agent-3 analysis, Option B `run_stream_with_breaker`): new module `SRC/neothd/src/providers/circuit_breaker_stream.rs` exposes `StreamGuard` (pin-project'd, settles permit on first terminal event: done-chunk = success, err-chunk = failure, exhausted-without-done = failure, premature consumer drop = failure via `OwnedPermit::drop`) + `run_stream_with_breaker(provider_id, fut)` helper. All 3 `Provider::stream` impls wrapped: `claude_cli.rs`, `local_qwen.rs`, `ouro/adapter.rs`. `Cargo.toml` adds `pin-project = "1"`. 6 new tests cover success/failure/drop/exhaust/open-rejection/construction-failure paths. Open circuit rejection surfaces as `anyhow!("circuit breaker open for {name}: ...")` identical to the `complete()` semantics.

- [x] **GR-06** Document `OnSessionStart` `Block` semantics (logged, does not stop daemon). — F6 — XS — ✅ 2026-05-27 Session 27. New 10-line doc-comment block above the OnSessionStart hook dispatch in `cli::serve::run_serve` documents the intentional design: at this stage every subsystem (WAL writer / indexer / channels / cron / HTTP listener) has already spawned, so a `StageOutcome::Block` from a single hook tearing all of those down would surface as a half-booted daemon that never reaches its idle wait loop. Block is therefore logged via `warn!(hook, reason, ...)` + ignored — operators see the intent without it being load-bearing on liveness. Inline comment on the `Block` arm cross-references the module-doc rationale. The Block-aborts-boot variant is intentionally deferred until an earlier hook stage exists OR an operator surfaces a real need; deferring documented inline so a future maintainer doesn't re-invent the missing-stage workaround.

Closed as accepted risk via single-source-of-truth invariant. The segment writer pins `{:06}.wal` zero-padded filenames at the two emit sites (`wal::writer` `format!("{:06}.wal", next_seq)` + `cli::wal` `format!("{:06}.wal", seq)`); with that invariant lexicographic sort == numeric sort for sequences 0..=999_999, which is ~125 GB at the 128 MB segment cap (well past any realistic operator lifetime). 10-line doc-comment on `cli::verify::list_segments` documents the invariant + names the writer test that pins the format contract (`is_zero_padded_segment_name`). Defensive numeric parse path NOT added — it'd duplicate work the writer side already enforces, and a future writer-side regression would break the test pin before reaching this sort site.

New `policy::PolicyConfig` fields `startup_audit_scan_paths: Vec<PathBuf>` + `forbid_inline_tokens_in_remotes: bool` (both serde-default empty/false so existing policy.yaml files inherit no-op behaviour — opt-in). New `daemon::startup_credential_audit` module with pure-fn `run_credential_scan(scan_paths, check_git_remotes) -> Vec<CredentialFinding>` over operator-listed paths. **Key design choice — pattern source-of-truth**: instead of duplicating the secret-shape regex table, the scanner DIFFs `security::redact::redact_text(line)` against the original line — every `[REDACTED:<kind>]` marker in the output is one hit. Means a future tune of redact patterns automatically tightens audit too; can't drift. File scanner: 1-based line numbers; missing path = quiet skip (operator may list ~/.bashrc that doesn't exist on this host); permissions/binary errors = quiet skip per-file (audit must never crash boot). Directory walk is ONE LEVEL only — operators who want recursion list each subdir explicitly. Git remote scanner: shells out to `git remote -v`, parses inline `https://user:token@host/...` URLs via pure-fn `inline_token_excerpt(url)` (only `https://`-scoped; ssh URLs don't carry inline tokens). 8-char excerpt + ellipsis so the audit output doesn't itself become a leak. `format_finding(&f)` gives operator-readable line for `neoth doctor` + warn-log emission. Wired into `cli/serve.rs` section 5a-creds right after the kanban reaper; silent no-op when no scan-paths + flag-off; warn-only on findings (never fails boot — operators legitimately keep API keys in shell rc files). 13 tests: empty-scan-list / missing-path-skipped / anthropic_key detection / github_pat detection / aws_access_key detection / clean-file no-findings / one-level-only walk (nested file NOT found) / 1-based line numbers / inline_token_excerpt finds-password / inline_token_excerpt none-for-ssh / inline_token_excerpt none-without-token / inline_token_excerpt none-when-password-empty / format_finding includes-line / format_finding omits-zero-line (git-remote findings).

- [x] **QU-01** SmallCode EarlyStopDetector — repetition-loop tail-scan + patch-spiral counter (4 failures → full-rewrite) + greeting-regression detector. `coding/retry_policy.rs` ~220 LOC. Today every failure → silent `Blocked`. — F5 — S 3-4h — **Phase 2 dispatcher wire-in shipped 2026-05-27 Session 28** (same session as Phase 1). `coding::dispatcher::dispatch_session_with_apply` now constructs a `PatchSpiralTracker::new()` per-session next to the existing `WorkerRetryPolicy::new()`, threads `&mut patch_spiral` through every `handle_retryable_failure` call site, calls `patch_spiral.record(task, false)` on every empty-outcome / worker-error / apply-failure path AND `patch_spiral.record(task, true)` on every successful apply-or-no-apply completion (so a task that recovers after one bad attempt resets the counter). Inside `handle_retryable_failure`, BEFORE the existing retry-policy `should_retry` check, two early-stop bypasses fire: (1) `is_greeting_regression(diagnosis)` OR `is_greeting_regression(partial_outcome.summary)` OR `is_greeting_regression(partial_outcome.patch_text)` → warn-log with `early_stop=greeting_regression` marker + transition straight to Blocked + early return (skips retry rotation entirely because rotating SplitFile/OneError/RewriteSection on a refusal just burns budget); (2) `patch_spiral.is_spiraling(task)` → warn-log with `early_stop=patch_spiral + consecutive_failures=N` marker + transition straight to Blocked + early return. Both bypasses honour redacted diagnosis (security::redact::redact_text runs first so leaked secrets in error strings stay out of the warn-log). 3 wire-in tests in `dispatcher::tests`: greeting_regression_bypasses_retry_rotation_and_blocks / empty_refusal_outcome_triggers_greeting_regression_bypass / repeated_empty_outcome_lands_blocked_via_retry_ceiling. **Repetition-loop detector wire-in — Phase 3 SHIPPED 2026-05-27 Session 28**: `dispatch_session_with_apply` now threads a per-task `HashMap<KanbanTaskId, Vec<String>>` recent-output ring next to the patch-spiral tracker. Every failure path (apply-rejection / empty-outcome / worker-error) records the attempt's text via `worker_output_text(&o)` (newline-joins `summary` + `patch_text` so two attempts differing only in one surface stay distinct) into the ring via `record_recent_output` (caps at `REPETITION_RING_CAP = 8`, drains oldest past cap). `recent_output_refs` borrows the ring as `Vec<&str>` for the detector. `handle_retryable_failure` gains a `recent_outputs: &[&str]` param + a third early-stop check after greeting + spiral: `is_repetition_loop(recent_outputs)` → warn-log `early_stop=repetition_loop` + straight to Blocked. Productive completion (`remove(&task.task_id)`) clears the ring so a re-queued task starts fresh. Detector fires at attempt 3 (REPETITION_LOOP_MIN_SAMPLES) — one before the patch-spiral ceiling of 4 — so an identical-output wedge bails one attempt sooner than a varied-but-failing one. 4 new helper tests: worker_output_text-joins-both-surfaces / record_recent_output-caps-at-capacity (oldest evicted) / recent_output_refs-empty-for-unknown-task / recent_output_refs-round-trips-into-detector (2 samples below floor → false, 3 identical → true). **All three QU-01 detectors now wired into the live dispatcher** (greeting-regression + patch-spiral Phase 2 + repetition-loop Phase 3). **No-apply greeting-regression edge closed 2026-05-27 Session 28** (Alex harte-Kritik #1): a refusal that arrives as non-empty `patch_text` is STRUCTURALLY review-ready, so the pre-fix dispatcher promoted it to Review on the no-`--apply` path (the apply path caught it on git-apply rejection, but no-apply never inspected content). New match arm in `dispatch_session_with_apply` BEFORE the `Ok(o) if o.review_ready()` arm: `Ok(o) if o.review_ready() && (is_greeting_regression(&o.patch_text) || is_greeting_regression(&o.summary))` routes the refusal into the failure path → `handle_retryable_failure`'s greeting-regression check fires → Blocked, not Review, regardless of `--apply`. No false positives on real patches (a legit `diff --git ...` patch_text contains diff markers so `is_greeting_regression` returns false). Test `refusal_disguised_as_patch_blocks_even_without_apply` replaces the prior test that documented the old leak-through behaviour. (Phase 1 substrate: 14 pure-fn tests + `is_repetition_loop` + `is_greeting_regression` + `PatchSpiralTracker` shipped earlier this session.) New `coding::early_stop` module exports three pure-fn detectors + one tracker: (1) `is_repetition_loop(recent_outputs)` returns true when the last `REPETITION_LOOP_MIN_SAMPLES = 3` worker outputs are byte-identical after whitespace collapse (catches model wedges where the worker re-emits the same reply with drifting indentation — common with tool-template re-renders); empty-tail explicitly excluded so the patch-spiral detector below owns the no-output case. (2) `is_greeting_regression(output)` lowercase + trim → check first 200 chars against 14 bilingual EN+DE greeting/refusal prefixes (`"sorry, i can't"` / `"es tut mir leid"` / `"als ki kann ich"` / ...) AND check the full output contains no work markers (`<patch>` / `\`\`\`diff` / `\`\`\`rust` / `\`\`\`python` / `\`\`\`ts` / `\`\`\`js` / `--- a/` / `+++ b/`) so polite-preamble-then-real-work is NOT flagged; 200-char head window keeps the check tight against long replies where line-1 greeting is style not regression. (3) `PatchSpiralTracker { consecutive_failures: HashMap<KanbanTaskId, u32>, ceiling }` per-task counter — `record(task, applied_ok=true)` resets, `record(task, false)` increments, `is_spiraling(task)` trips at `DEFAULT_PATCH_SPIRAL_CEILING = 4`; ceiling clamped to `.max(1)` so `with_ceiling(0)` doesn't degenerate to "spiral on first failure". `Default` impl + `Clone` so the dispatcher can stash an `Arc<Mutex<…>>`-friendly tracker per session without ceremony. 14 tests: minimum-samples floor / tail-identical fires / whitespace-drift tolerance / productive-variety ignored / all-empty excluded / EN refusal / DE refusal / preamble-plus-work NOT flagged / empty-output NOT flagged / non-greeting-preamble NOT flagged / 200-char-window respected / spiral increment+reset / spiral trips at ceiling / spiral per-task independence / ceiling floor clamp / constant drift guards. **Phase 2 deferred**: dispatcher wire-in (calling the detectors in `coding::dispatcher::handle_retryable_failure` before retry-policy invocation) needs the dispatcher to thread recent-outputs state — currently only the most-recent outcome is in scope. Cross-worker correlation + confidence-decay detectors are explicitly out-of-scope per inline doc-comment.

Two of the four deliverables already shipped via the smallcode-audit port + P-04 + HO-06 (pattern-based secret detection in `PATTERNS` + the field-name deny-list), so the "today: .env + Bearer land raw in WAL" framing was the pre-ship state; this commit lands the remaining two, names the deny-list per spec, and adds the centralized surface. **(1) `ALWAYS_REDACT_KEYS`** — the previously-private `SENSITIVE` const lifted to `pub const ALWAYS_REDACT_KEYS: &[&str]` (spec name); `is_sensitive_field_name` now iterates it; pinned by `qu_04_always_redact_keys_is_lowercase_and_drives_field_guard` (every entry lowercase + recognised by the guard). **(2) `strip_ansi(&str) -> Cow`** — LazyLock regex matching CSI (`\x1b[…`), OSC (`\x1b]…`, BEL/ST-terminated) and generic two-byte escapes; alternation order is load-bearing (CSI/OSC before the catch-all so `\x1b]` opens an OSC instead of being eaten as a lone escape). Borrows when escape-free (no alloc on the clean hot path). **(3) path-traversal block** — `contains_path_traversal` (cheap `../`+`..\` substring branch) + `neutralize_path_traversal(&str) -> Cow` (collapses each run of `../`/`..\` to one `[REDACTED:path_traversal]` marker, module-consistent wire form). Bare `..` with no separator is intentionally NOT matched (prose "wait.." / version "1.0..2.0"). **(4) `sanitize_tool_output(&str) -> String`** — the centralized canonical surface: `strip_ansi` THEN `redact_text`. ANSI-first is correctness-critical: a colourised secret (`\x1b[31msk-…\x1b[0m`) has escape bytes wedged inside the token, breaking the `\b` word-boundary the secret regexes anchor on — strip first so the pattern sees a clean token (test `qu_04_sanitize_tool_output_strips_ansi_then_redacts_secret`). **Design choice — traversal neutralisation is deliberately NOT in `sanitize_tool_output`**: cargo/rustc diagnostics legitimately print relative paths (`--> ../src/foo.rs`), and blanket-rewriting them would corrupt the audit trail (drift-guard test `qu_04_sanitize_tool_output_preserves_relative_compiler_paths`); `neutralize_path_traversal` is an opt-in boundary tool for callers handling UNTRUSTED paths. `redact_text` behaviour is unchanged — HO-06's startup credential scanner DIFFs its output, so the secret-pattern pass had to stay byte-identical. **Wiring**: `coding::dispatcher::handle_retryable_failure` (the diagnosis sanitisation point, ~L798) `redact_text(diagnosis)` → `sanitize_tool_output(diagnosis)` — one canonical pass covering every downstream consumer of `diagnosis` (the 3 QU-01 early-stop log markers + the QU-05 re-injection hint that lands in the task description + WAL + the Blocked-reason emit); cargo/test output arrives ANSI-colourised so this is the real ANSI-bearing surface. The fully-qualified `redact_text` call at the WAL blocked-emit helper stays (no ANSI there). 13 new redact tests (34 module total) + dispatcher 31 green; clippy `--lib --tests -- -D warnings` clean; verified local MSVC build.

New `EVENT_TYPE_PROVIDER_FALLBACK_ATTEMPTED = 0x25` (provider band 0x20..=0x2F, verified-free + band-asserted + name-registry + SC-01a wired-guard passes), emitted at the hop-decision site inside `FallbackProvider::complete` once per ACTUAL attempt (a 429-backoff SKIP takes no hop → no frame). Payload `{from_provider, to_provider, reason:"quota_429", hop, prompt_hash_xxh3, ts_unix}` — `prompt_hash_xxh3` uses the SAME u64 xxh3 encoding as the `PROVIDER_REQUEST` (0x20) frame so an operator correlates the hop with the turn. **Writer-layering decision:** `FallbackProvider` gained `wal_writer: Option<WalWriterHandle>` threaded through `fallback_chain_from_config`. The DAEMON (`cli/serve.rs`, writer live at chain-build) passes it → the unattended channel/cron path (where prompts actually wander) gets the durable frame; the CLI one-shot (`cli/chat.rs`, writer created below the provider in the call stack + operator present) passes `None`. - [x] **ADV-13** Deterministic pre-extraction timestamp normalization stage — NLP parses relative time expressions ("3 years ago") against conversation's actual `hlc_ns` before LLM call. — F4 — S 2d — ✅ 2026-05-28 Session 28b. This is the exact "future rule-NLP layer" `profile::timestamp_check` (M1) explicitly defers to in its v0.1 doc ("Relative time phrases … left for a future rule-NLP layer"). New pure module `profile/relative_time.rs`: `normalize_relative_dates(text, reference: NaiveDate) -> Cow` rewrites deterministic relative expressions to absolute `yyyy-mm-dd` anchored on a fixed reference (NO wall-clock `now()`, NO LLM — preserves the extractor's G.1 determinism). Rule families (bilingual EN+DE): EN `<n> <unit> ago` / DE `vor <n> <unit>` (past) · EN+DE `in <n> <unit>` (future) · EN `last|next <unit>` / DE `letzte|nächste <unit>` (single-unit) · single tokens `today/yesterday/tomorrow/heute/gestern/vorgestern`. `classify_unit` prefix-maps every EN+DE inflection to Day/Week/Month/Year; Month/Year use chrono calendar arithmetic (`Months`) not 30/365-day approximations. **Conservative by design**: bare German "morgen" deliberately NOT matched (collides with "morning"/"guten Morgen"); unrecognised expressions left verbatim (safe failure = LLM resolves as before). Result year clamped to `[1900,2200]` via `sane_year` — same bounds `timestamp_check.rs` already enforces, so an absurd "9999 years ago" is left verbatim instead of emitting `-7973-05-28` (cross-module consistency; a normalised date round-trips through M1's window check). `needs_normalization` pre-check borrows the input untouched on the common no-relative-expression path. **Wiring**: `profile::extract::render_user_prompt` now runs `relative_time::normalize_segment(&scrubbed, seg.segment.ts_ns)` per segment AFTER boundary-char scrubbing — each segment's claims anchor on ITS real conversation timestamp, not the model's training "now". Per-segment ts_ns keeps the render deterministic (fixed input → fixed prompt). 20 relative_time tests (EN+DE per rule family + calendar-month arithmetic + borrow-on-clean + overflow/absurd-count safety + ns→date + bad-timestamp passthrough + determinism) + extract 28 still green (no snapshot/determinism break from the wire-in); clippy `--lib --tests -- -D warnings` clean. Verified local MSVC build.

(2) `profile/runner.rs:397` — `is_local_inference_provider` matched `"local_qwen" | "hermes" | "openclaw"` → `"local_qwen" | "local_ouro"`; under H3 privacy this means `local_ouro` profile-extraction was being classified as a CLOUD provider and tripping the V10-07 cloud-provider privacy warn (the two privacy-classification tests updated to assert `local_ouro` is local). **Comment-only fixes:** `coding/provider_worker.rs:4` head doc provider list; `cli/hemispheres.rs` `--provider` + `--endpoint` arg help (40/51) and the `run_set` unknown-provider error string (164) — all three now list the accurate `from_str`-accepted set (claude_cli, anthropic_api, openai_api, openai_compat, gemini_api, local_qwen, local_ouro, aws_bedrock, azure_openai). **Public-release hygiene:** `providers/known_endpoints.rs:70` leaked an operator-specific absolute path (`c:/Users/<operator>/.../openclawfix/AIRFORCE_MODELS.json`) into a doc comment of a file destined for the public The-Geek-Freaks/NEOTH push — scrubbed to a neutral "offline provider-catalogue research dump" description. **Left intact (verified accurate, NOT drift):** `ToolFormat::Hermes` (a real Hermes-Pro XML tool-call format, not the provider); the `OPENCLAW_*` harness env-var scrubbing in `claude_cli.rs` (a real security feature against Alex's gateway leaking vars to the model); the openclaw-bonjour watchdog design-lineage citations in `cluster/*`; the "Hermes-adapted" coding-workflow lineage in `coding/mod.rs`; the groundtruth foreign-agent import formats in `cli/groundtruth.rs` (hermes/openclaw ARE real import sources); and the `config/inference.rs` removal/correction notes. Verified: clippy `--lib --tests` 0 warnings, `providers::cost` + `profile::runner` suites green (incl. the new `local_ouro` assertions), fmt 0 diffs. **Adversarial-review follow-up (4-lens review-workflow → 16 findings → 3 confirmed, 13 refuted; Session 30):** the review caught that the GR-17 sweep was **INCOMPLETE** — it fixed cost.rs + runner.rs but the same `== "local_qwen"` drift lived at FIVE more sites, two of them privacy-relevant. The fix extracts the canonical `crate::providers::is_local_provider(name) -> matches!(name, "local_qwen" | "local_ouro")` as the SINGLE enumeration point and routes every "is it local" guard through it: `cli/chat.rs` quota pre-flight (803) + WAL-audit `is_local_inference` (966, the LOCAL_INFERENCE_START/END frames were silently skipped for local_ouro — broken audit chain) + quota `record_success` (1632); `cli/privacy.rs` provider-classification (89) + learn-provider-classification (115) — the latter two had labelled local_ouro as **"CLOUD"** with a false "posts your prompt to local_ouro's servers" privacy warning for an on-device model; `providers/quota.rs::backoff_remaining_for` (202); and `profile/runner.rs::is_local_inference_provider` now delegates to the canonical fn. **Verify-first kept `doctor.rs::check_local_qwen_weights` (858) keyed to local_qwen alone** — it checks the Qwen weight cache specifically, not "is it local" (local_ouro has its own Ouro checkpoint). 5 new tests (canonical `is_local_provider` matrix incl. empty-string + cloud rejects / quota `backoff_remaining_for("local_ouro")→None` mirroring the local_qwen guard / privacy `local_ouro`→LOCAL-ONLY-info as both default + learn provider). Verified: full lib **6189/0**, clippy `--bins --lib --tests` 0 warnings, fmt clean.

The only thing buildable WITHOUT the streaming refactor is a 1-send + 1-final-edit where **the edit re-writes the message to the SAME text** (no progressive update) — a cosmetic no-op that would still introduce live-pipeline `Ok(None)` hazards (bypasses the Telegram MarkdownV2 retry path + reorders the CHANNEL_EGRESS emit). - [x] **AUDIT-RPC-01** Daemon-RPC for one-shot audit intents (operator-flagged 2026-06-02; structural) — when the daemon holds the WAL writer, one-shot CLI tools SKIP WAL emission (no writer handle). They forward an audit-intent to the running daemon so a `neoth os launch`/`fs`/`lease`/etc. run while `neoth serve` is up still lands its audit frames. Closes the "live daemon = silent one-shots" gap. — F2 — M — 🧭 **3-lens design panel (architecture/security/portability + synthesis) → BUILD-NOW. ✅ DAEMON MECHANISM SHIPPED 2026-06-02 Session 35** (`daemon/audit_rpc.rs`, net-new). Heavily reuses existing infra (the panel found it): `n8n_api::{constant_time_token_eq,extract_bearer_token}` + `n8n_api::auth::AuthCooldown` + `wal::compaction::{write_key_securely,maybe_unwrap_dpapi}` + the hand-rolled `daemon/healthz.rs` loopback-listener shape + `WalWriterHandle::clone`. **Transport:** dedicated loopback-TCP `127.0.0.1:0` (OS-assigned), separate from healthz, gated `freedom.yaml::audit_rpc.enabled` (default OFF struct-level / wizard-on); port advertised in `~/.neoth/audit_rpc.port` sidecar (carries port + daemon PID + 8-char token-hint, NEVER the full token). **Auth (anti-audit-poisoning):** per-boot 32-byte bearer token (base64url, fresh each daemon start → captured token dies on restart) written 0600/DPAPI+DACL; loopback peer-guard at accept; `constant_time_token_eq` + 5-strike `AuthCooldown`; **compile-time event-type allowlist** (`ALLOWED_CLIENT_EVENT_TYPES` = the 9 one-shot codes `0xA5..=0xAD` only — a `const`, not a runtime toggle); body cap 4096. Endpoint `POST /audit` → daemon `writer.append`s the forwarded frame (single-writer + HMAC chain preserved) + WAL **`0xAE AUDIT_RPC_ACCEPT`**; refusals → **`0xAF AUDIT_RPC_REJECT`** (auth failures NOT recorded — forged-frame paradox + spam). Client `try_post_audit_frame` (allowlisted in `no_outbound_network.rs` — loopback same-host IPC, not egress). **Security review (3-lens-grounded, 0 crit/high):** 2 MEDIUM fixed pre-commit — (1) **stale-sidecar token-disclosure** (crashed daemon → recycled port → token leaked to wrong process): sidecar now carries the daemon PID + the client refuses a sidecar whose PID isn't alive (`pidfile::pid_is_alive` made `pub(crate)`), + `remove_sidecar` on daemon start clears crash-leftovers; (2) **slowloris/unbounded-spawn**: 5s per-connection timeout + bounded `Semaphore(32)` (excess connections dropped). 2 LOW documented (windows `write_key_securely` brief-DACL-window = PRE-EXISTING shared-helper, DPAPI-mitigated; `AuthCooldown` no-eviction = non-issue on the single-key loopback path). 10 audit_rpc tests (allowlist / token+sidecar round-trip / sidecar-guard / wrong-token→401-no-frame / blocked-type→422+reject / valid→append+accept / **end-to-end client round-trip** / no-sidecar→unavailable / **dead-pid-sidecar→stale-refused**). lib 6590/0, clippy clean. ✅ **Commit 2 — os_tools CONSUMER WIRING SHIPPED 2026-06-02 Session 35** (`eb87664` follow-on): the 3 os_tools one-shot consumers now FORWARD their audit frames when a daemon is live instead of silently dropping them. New **`os_tools::AuditSink`** enum (`None` / `Writer(&handle)` / `DaemonRpc(&home)`) replaces the gates' old `Option<&WalWriterHandle>` param — single source of truth for the local-append-vs-daemon-forward routing via one `dispatch_frame` (NO duplicated payload-building, no drift). `read_os_file`/`write_os_file`/`launch_os_app` take `AuditSink`; `cli/fs.rs` (read+write) + `cli/os.rs` (launch) pass `DaemonRpc(&home)` when `live_daemon_pid` is Some (the liveness gate ALSO closes the stale-sidecar vector at the caller, belt-and-suspenders with the client's own pid check), `Writer(&w)` when they own a one-shot writer, `None` only when no daemon AND writer-open failed. Best-effort: a disabled/unreachable listener → frame dropped, the gated action STILL succeeds (proven by `daemon_rpc_sink_without_listener_is_graceful_noop`). lib 6591/0, clippy clean, no os_tools test regressed. **✅ **ALL 5 REMAINING ONE-SHOT COMMANDS WIRED 2026-06-03 Session 36 — AUDIT-RPC-01 CLOSED for v1.0.** `lease`/`ingest`/`models`/`recall_score`/`update` now FORWARD their audit frames over the loopback channel when a daemon owns the WAL (was: silent skip). The `ALLOWED_CLIENT_EVENT_TYPES` allowlist grew 11→17 with the 6 new one-shot codes (`0x2C INGEST_EXTRACTED`/`0x2D EMBED_PERSISTED`/`0x3E EVAL_CRITICAL_DIVERGENCE`/`0xD2 SELF_UPDATE_APPLIED`/`0xD7 MODEL_DOWNLOAD_START`/`0xD8 MODEL_DOWNLOAD_COMPLETE`) — then **→20 (Session 36 P0)** with `0xF5 MEMORY_TRANSFER_EXPORTED` (transfer-hardening), `0x9B IDENTITY_MERGED` (identity-merge P0), and `0xC8 TODO_WRITE` (unified ExternalTaskWrite gate P0), so identity-merge + external-task-write also forward instead of skipping on a live daemon; each emit site builds its payload up front, then either forwards via `try_post_audit_frame` (daemon live, best-effort — a disabled/unreachable listener falls through to no-frame, the action already succeeded) or opens its one-shot writer (no daemon). `cli/profile.rs::seed-baseline` stays a hard daemon-blocker by design (not a forwarding candidate). `recall-score` reports audit-complete on daemon-live (a disabled listener is the operator's config choice, not an audit failure). The audit_rpc module is already a directory (`daemon/audit_rpc/{mod,server,client,sidecar,token,tests}.rs` — refinement #3 done). allowlist test updated (`len()==17` + the 6 new codes asserted-allowed). lib 6648/0, clippy 0. **ONLY DEFERRED (Alex "nicht P0", long-term): Refinement #2** — swap loopback-TCP for a Unix domain socket / Windows named pipe (filesystem-perm-gated, stronger than TCP-loopback). Post-v1.0 enhancement; the TCP-loopback + per-boot-token + allowlist + pid-guard mechanism is the shipped v1.0 transport. ✅ **Refinement #1 (required-audit fail-closed) SHIPPED 2026-06-02 Session 35** (`68efc68`): `freedom.yaml::audit_rpc.required_for_oneshot_permission_events` (default false) → when set + daemon-live + listener unreachable, `neoth fs read/write` / `os launch` / `autonomy set` REFUSE before acting (no permission action un-audited). `audit_rpc::{is_reachable (sidecar+pid-alive), enforce_required_audit}` + pre-flight in the 3 consumers + 2 tests. **Refinements still open: #3** audit_rpc.rs (now ~810 LOC) → split into `daemon/audit_rpc/{mod,token,sidecar,server,client,tests}.rs` (PURE mechanical refactor, no behavior change — keep all pub paths via `mod.rs` re-export; deferred from the S35 marathon tail because a large module-split deserves a fresh focused pass, not the 18th commit on a flaky box with 20-min full-suite cycles). **#2 (DEFERRED, Alex "nicht P0"):** swap the loopback-TCP transport for a Unix domain socket (`~/.neoth/audit.sock`, filesystem-perm-gated) on unix + a Windows named pipe (`\\.\pipe\neoth-audit-rpc-<sid>`) — stronger than TCP-loopback but explicitly long-term. — (v1.x)

### Audit-RPC strict V2 supersession (2026-07-28)

The historical AUDIT-RPC-01 entry above records the original loopback-TCP
delivery, but that transport is no longer the current contract. R3-17 replaces
it with kernel-authenticated same-user OS IPC: an owner-private Unix socket or
current-user-only Windows named pipe, discovered through the strict bounded
`~/.neoth/audit_rpc.endpoint.v2.json` record. There is no TCP fallback, port
advertisement, or token hint; the per-boot bearer remains defense in depth.

Plus an operator-resettable **working baseline** (`DriftBaseline` + `save/load/reset_drift_baseline` atomic `.json` IO at `~/.neoth/profile_drift_baseline.json`) — kept DISTINCT from the immutable exactly-once `0xB3` migration anchor so `reset` doesn't fight the append-only WAL. **(2) `freedom.yaml::drift_alert`** config block — `DriftAlertConfig{enabled: bool (default false), threshold: f64 (default 0.25)}`. **(3) `neoth profile drift {report, baseline, reset}` CLI**: `report` (default) loads the working baseline → falls back to the `0xB3` anchor (`scan_for_baseline_snapshot_full` deserialises the full snapshot, not just the id) → diffs against current `idx_profile` active claims → renders added/removed/retained/ratio + flags when `drift_alert.enabled && ratio > threshold`; `baseline` (re)captures the working baseline to now; `reset` clears it (falls back to anchor). 12 tests (9 baseline_diff pure: identical/added+removed/empty-NaN-safe/total-turnover/dedup/file round-trip/no-tmp-leak/reset-idempotent/malformed-error + 3 config: SC-10-adjacent default + threshold + YAML round-trip). clippy `--lib --tests -- -D warnings` clean; fmt clean. **HO-09b — 4th deliverable (daemon cron) shipped 2026-05-30 Session 29.** New `EVENT_TYPE_PROFILE_DRIFT_ALERT = 0xBA` (profile band 0xB0..=0xBF, first free slot after 0xB9; band-membership compile-assert + name-registry + `all_event_codes_are_unique` array all updated). New `daemon/drift_alert_cron.rs` mirroring `doctor_cron`: pure-fn `run_drift_alert_tick(home, &DriftAlertConfig, writer)` + `spawn_drift_alert_cron_loop(config, home, writer) -> Option<JoinHandle>` (returns None when `drift_alert.enabled = false` → no idle task; `DEFAULT_CRON_INTERVAL_SECS = 6h`). The tick reuses the SAME baseline-resolution as `neoth profile drift report` via a new shared seam `cli::profile::compute_drift_against_baseline(home, db_path, wal_dir)` (working baseline → 0xB3 anchor fallback; `Ok(None)` on fresh install so the cron skips silently) — `current_active_claim_hashes` + `scan_for_baseline_snapshot_full` lifted to `pub(crate)`. Emits `0xBA` ONLY when `report.is_over(threshold)` (every frame is operator-actionable, not hourly "still fine" noise); payload `{ drift_ratio, threshold, added_count, removed_count, baseline_source, ts_unix }` — no claim hashes (signal not dump). Wired into `cli/serve.rs` (spawn after the G-02 cron + shutdown abort-before-writer-drain, mirroring doctor_cron). SC-01a satisfied — the 0xBA constant is referenced at the emit site so `tests/wal_emit_sites.rs` sees it WIRED, not defined-dead. 6 cron tests (spawn None/Some by enabled + tick no-baseline-skip + over-threshold-emits-0xBA + under-threshold-no-alert + default-interval pin). Verified: full `cargo test -p neothd` green (6128 lib tests), clippy `--lib --tests` 0 warnings, fmt 0 diffs. **HO-09 fully closed.** **Adversarial-review follow-up (Session 29, 4-lens workflow → 3 confirmed MEDIUM, 1 refuted FP):** (1) made the cron interval operator-tunable — new `freedom.yaml::drift_alert.interval_secs` field (serde-default 6h via `DEFAULT_DRIFT_ALERT_INTERVAL_SECS`) + `DriftAlertConfig::interval_duration()` clamping to a 60s floor on the CONFIG value (the prior `.max(60)` clamped the compile-time constant — vacuous + a misleading "future misconfigured 0" guard); (2) routed `run_drift`'s Report arm through `compute_drift_against_baseline` so the CLI report + the cron genuinely share one baseline-resolution seam (the doc claimed it but the CLI had an inline copy — duplication eliminated); (3) the emit test now decodes the on-disk `0xBA` payload + asserts `baseline_source`/`added_count`/`removed_count`/`drift_ratio` against an ASYMMETRIC setup (2 current claims vs 1-hash baseline) so a silent serialization regression on the operator-facing WAL signal is caught.
